from sqlglot import parse_one, exp


# -------------------------------
# helpers
# -------------------------------

def extract_tables(tree):
    """
    alias -> {table, db, type}
    """
    tables = {}

    # --- CTE ---
    with_expr = tree.find(exp.With)
    if with_expr:
        for cte in with_expr.expressions:
            alias = cte.alias
            tables[alias] = {
                "table": alias,
                "db": None,
                "type": "CTE"
            }

    # --- TABLES + SUBQUERY ---
    for node in tree.walk():
        if isinstance(node, exp.Table):
            alias = node.alias_or_name
            tables[alias] = {
                "table": node.name,
                "db": node.db,
                "type": "TABLE"
            }

        elif isinstance(node, exp.Subquery):
            alias = node.alias
            if alias:
                tables[alias] = {
                    "table": None,
                    "db": None,
                    "type": "SUBQUERY"
                }

    return tables


def is_column(node):
    return isinstance(node, exp.Column)


def extract_column(node):
    return node.table, node.name


def extract_conditions(expr):
    """
    рекурсивно достаём только COLUMN = COLUMN
    """
    result = []

    if expr is None:
        return result

    if isinstance(expr, exp.EQ):
        l, r = expr.left, expr.right

        if is_column(l) and is_column(r):
            result.append((extract_column(l), extract_column(r)))

    elif isinstance(expr, (exp.And, exp.Or)):
        result += extract_conditions(expr.left)
        result += extract_conditions(expr.right)

    else:
        for arg in expr.args.values():
            if isinstance(arg, exp.Expression):
                result += extract_conditions(arg)
            elif isinstance(arg, list):
                for item in arg:
                    if isinstance(item, exp.Expression):
                        result += extract_conditions(item)

    return result


# -------------------------------
# MAIN
# -------------------------------

def extract_joins(sql: str):
    tree = parse_one(sql, read="mysql")

    alias_map = extract_tables(tree)
    results = []

    for join in tree.find_all(exp.Join):
        on_expr = join.args.get("on")

        conditions = extract_conditions(on_expr)

        if not conditions:
            continue

        # определяем алиасы таблиц из условий
        aliases = set()
        for (l, r) in conditions:
            aliases.add(l[0])
            aliases.add(r[0])

        if len(aliases) != 2:
            # пропускаем сложные случаи (3+ таблицы)
            continue

        a1, a2 = list(aliases)

        t1 = alias_map.get(a1, {})
        t2 = alias_map.get(a2, {})

        # --- формируем связи ---
        links = []
        for i, (l, r) in enumerate(conditions, start=1):
            if l[0] == a1:
                left_col = l[1]
                right_col = r[1]
            else:
                left_col = r[1]
                right_col = l[1]

            links.append({
                f"связь{i}": {
                    "поле1": left_col,
                    "поле2": right_col,
                    "предикат": "ON"
                }
            })

        # --- isOnlyTables ---
        is_only_tables = (
            t1.get("type") == "TABLE" and
            t2.get("type") == "TABLE"
        )

        result = {
            "таблица1": t1.get("table"),
            "алиас1": a1,
            "таблица2": t2.get("table"),
            "алиас2": a2,
            "кол-во связей": len(links),
            "join_side": join.side,
            "join_kind": join.kind,
            "isOnlyTables": is_only_tables
        }

        # добавляем связи внутрь
        for link in links:
            result.update(link)

        results.append(result)

    return results








def analyze_joins(sql: str):
    tree = sqlglot.parse_one(sql)

    # 1. Собираем все алиасы
    sources = [tree.find(exp.From).this] + [j.this for j in tree.find_all(exp.Join)]
    aliases = {}
    for src in sources:
        alias = src.alias or src.name
        full  = f"{src.args.get('db', '')}.{src.name}".lstrip(".")
        aliases[alias] = full

    # 2. Собираем связи
    links = []
    for join in tree.find_all(exp.Join):
        on_clause = join.args.get("on")
        if not on_clause:
            continue
        for eq in on_clause.find_all(exp.EQ):
            links.append((
                f"{eq.left.table}.{eq.left.name}",
                f"{eq.right.table}.{eq.right.name}",
            ))

    print("=== Таблицы ===")
    for alias, full in aliases.items():
        print(f"  {alias:5} -> {full}")

    print("\n=== Связи ===")
    for l, r in links:
        print(f"  {l}  =  {r}")

analyze_joins(sql)














import sqlglot
from sqlglot import exp

sql = """
SELECT *
FROM dwh.fact_sales   AS fs
JOIN dwh.dim_product  AS dp ON fs.product_id = dp.id
JOIN dwh.dim_customer AS dc ON get_json_string(dp.json, "customer_id") = dc.id
"""

tree = sqlglot.parse_one(sql, dialect="starrocks")

def extract_columns_from_expr(node) -> list[exp.Column]:
    """Рекурсивно достаём все exp.Column из выражения (включая внутри функций)."""
    return list(node.find_all(exp.Column))

def analyze_joins(tree):
    links = []

    for join in tree.find_all(exp.Join):
        on_clause = join.args.get("on")
        if not on_clause:
            continue

        for eq in on_clause.find_all(exp.EQ):
            left_cols  = extract_columns_from_expr(eq.left)
            right_cols = extract_columns_from_expr(eq.right)

            # Определяем "сырое" представление каждой стороны
            left_repr  = eq.left.sql()   # читаемо: get_json_string(dp.json, 'customer_id')
            right_repr = eq.right.sql()  # dc.id

            # Уникальные алиасы таблиц, задействованных в каждой стороне
            left_tables  = list(dict.fromkeys(c.table for c in left_cols  if c.table))
            right_tables = list(dict.fromkeys(c.table for c in right_cols if c.table))

            links.append({
                "left_expr":   left_repr,
                "left_tables": left_tables,
                "right_expr":  right_repr,
                "right_tables": right_tables,
                # Пара для графа связей (все комбинации)
                "pairs": [(lt, rt) for lt in left_tables for rt in right_tables],
            })

    return links

for link in analyze_joins(tree):
    print(f"Выражение: {link['left_expr']}  =  {link['right_expr']}")
    print(f"Таблицы:   {link['left_tables']} <-> {link['right_tables']}")
    print(f"Связи:     {link['pairs']}")
    print()












import sqlglot
from sqlglot import exp

sql = """
SELECT *
FROM dwh.fact_sales   AS fs
JOIN dwh.dim_product  AS dp ON fs.product_id = dp.id
JOIN dwh.dim_customer AS dc ON get_json_string(dp.json, "customer_id") = dc.id
"""

tree = sqlglot.parse_one(sql, dialect="starrocks")

def build_alias_map(tree) -> dict[str, str]:
    sources = [tree.find(exp.From).this] + [j.this for j in tree.find_all(exp.Join)]
    result = {}
    for src in sources:
        alias = src.alias or src.name
        db    = src.args.get("db") or ""
        full  = f"{db}.{src.name}" if db else src.name
        result[alias] = full
    return result

def resolve_expr(node, alias_map: dict) -> str:
    """Заменяет алиасы таблиц на полные имена во всём выражении."""
    node = node.copy()
    for col in node.find_all(exp.Column):
        if col.table and col.table in alias_map:
            col.set("table", exp.Identifier(this=alias_map[col.table], quoted=False))
    return node.sql()

def analyze_joins(tree):
    alias_map = build_alias_map(tree)
    links = []

    for join in tree.find_all(exp.Join):
        on_clause = join.args.get("on")
        if not on_clause:
            continue

        for eq in on_clause.find_all(exp.EQ):
            left_cols  = list(eq.left.find_all(exp.Column))
            right_cols = list(eq.right.find_all(exp.Column))

            left_tables  = list(dict.fromkeys(
                alias_map.get(c.table, c.table) for c in left_cols  if c.table
            ))
            right_tables = list(dict.fromkeys(
                alias_map.get(c.table, c.table) for c in right_cols if c.table
            ))

            links.append({
                "left_expr":   resolve_expr(eq.left,  alias_map),
                "right_expr":  resolve_expr(eq.right, alias_map),
                "left_tables":  left_tables,
                "right_tables": right_tables,
                "pairs":        [(lt, rt) for lt in left_tables for rt in right_tables],
            })

    return links

for link in analyze_joins(tree):
    print(f"Выражение: {link['left_expr']}  =  {link['right_expr']}")
    print(f"Таблицы:   {link['left_tables']} <-> {link['right_tables']}")
    print(f"Связи:     {link['pairs']}")
    print()








import sqlglot
from sqlglot import exp

sql = """
WITH cte_sales AS (
    SELECT * FROM dwh.fact_sales
)
SELECT *
FROM cte_sales AS fs
JOIN dwh.dim_product  AS dp ON fs.product_id = dp.id
JOIN dwh.dim_customer AS dc ON get_json_string(dp.json, "customer_id") = dc.id
JOIN (SELECT id FROM dwh.dim_region) AS dr ON dc.region_id = dr.id
"""

tree = sqlglot.parse_one(sql, dialect="starrocks")

def build_alias_map(tree) -> dict[str, str]:
    # Имена CTE — исключаем
    cte_names = {
        cte.alias for cte in tree.find_all(exp.CTE)
    }

    sources = [tree.find(exp.From).this] + [j.this for j in tree.find_all(exp.Join)]
    result = {}

    for src in sources:
        alias = src.alias or src.name

        # Пропускаем подзапросы (Subquery внутри FROM/JOIN)
        if isinstance(src, exp.Subquery):
            continue

        # Пропускаем если имя таблицы совпадает с CTE
        if src.name in cte_names:
            continue

        db   = src.args.get("db") or ""
        full = f"{db}.{src.name}" if db else src.name
        result[alias] = full

    return result

def resolve_expr(node, alias_map: dict) -> str:
    node = node.copy()
    for col in node.find_all(exp.Column):
        if col.table and col.table in alias_map:
            col.set("table", exp.Identifier(this=alias_map[col.table], quoted=False))
    return node.sql()

def analyze_joins(tree):
    alias_map = build_alias_map(tree)
    links = []

    for join in tree.find_all(exp.Join):
        on_clause = join.args.get("on")
        if not on_clause:
            continue

        for eq in on_clause.find_all(exp.EQ):
            left_cols  = list(eq.left.find_all(exp.Column))
            right_cols = list(eq.right.find_all(exp.Column))

            # Фильтруем алиасы, которых нет в alias_map (CTE / Subquery)
            left_tables = list(dict.fromkeys(
                alias_map[c.table]
                for c in left_cols
                if c.table and c.table in alias_map
            ))
            right_tables = list(dict.fromkeys(
                alias_map[c.table]
                for c in right_cols
                if c.table and c.table in alias_map
            ))

            # Пропускаем связь если обе стороны не разрешились в реальные таблицы
            if not left_tables and not right_tables:
                continue

            links.append({
                "left_expr":   resolve_expr(eq.left,  alias_map),
                "right_expr":  resolve_expr(eq.right, alias_map),
                "left_tables":  left_tables  or ["<cte_or_subquery>"],
                "right_tables": right_tables or ["<cte_or_subquery>"],
                "pairs":        [(lt, rt) for lt in (left_tables  or ["<cte_or_subquery>"])
                                          for rt in (right_tables or ["<cte_or_subquery>"])],
            })

    return links

for link in analyze_joins(tree):
    print(f"Выражение: {link['left_expr']}  =  {link['right_expr']}")
    print(f"Таблицы:   {link['left_tables']} <-> {link['right_tables']}")
    print(f"Связи:     {link['pairs']}")
    print()

CTE-фильтр — собираем все имена через tree.find_all(exp.CTE) и исключаем таблицы, чьё src.name попало в этот список.
Subquery-фильтр — isinstance(src, exp.Subquery) отсекает инлайн-подзапросы в FROM/JOIN.
В analyze_joins — дополнительная проверка c.table in alias_map при формировании left_tables/right_tables: алиасы CTE и Subquery просто не попадают в результат, заменяясь на <cte_or_subquery> для наглядности (можно заменить на None или пропускать совсем).​​​​​​​​​​​​​​​​









Jira api

import requests
from requests.auth import HTTPBasicAuth

JIRA_URL = "https://your-domain.atlassian.net"
EMAIL    = "you@example.com"
TOKEN    = "your_api_token"

auth    = HTTPBasicAuth(EMAIL, TOKEN)
headers = {"Accept": "application/json"}


# ── 1. Получить одну задачу ──────────────────────────────────────────
def get_issue(issue_key: str) -> dict:
    """
    Возвращает поля задачи, включая description в rendered (HTML) виде.
    """
    url = f"{JIRA_URL}/rest/api/3/issue/{issue_key}"
    params = {
        "fields": "summary,description,status,assignee,comment",
        "expand": "renderedFields",   # ← даёт description как HTML
    }
    resp = requests.get(url, headers=headers, auth=auth, params=params)
    resp.raise_for_status()
    return resp.json()


# ── 2. Поиск задач по JQL ────────────────────────────────────────────
def search_issues(jql: str, fields: list[str] = None, max_results: int = 50) -> list[dict]:
    """
    Поиск через актуальный эндпоинт /rest/api/3/search/jql
    с поддержкой пагинации через nextPageToken.
    """
    url = f"{JIRA_URL}/rest/api/3/search/jql"
    fields = fields or ["summary", "description", "status", "assignee"]

    issues = []
    next_page_token = None

    while True:
        payload = {
            "jql": jql,
            "maxResults": max_results,
            "fields": fields,
            "expand": ["renderedFields"],
        }
        if next_page_token:
            payload["nextPageToken"] = next_page_token

        resp = requests.post(url, headers={**headers, "Content-Type": "application/json"},
                             auth=auth, json=payload)
        resp.raise_for_status()
        data = resp.json()

        issues.extend(data.get("issues", []))
        next_page_token = data.get("nextPageToken")

        if not next_page_token:
            break

    return issues


# ── 3. Извлечь HTML-описание и прогнать через наш парсер ─────────────
def get_issue_html(issue: dict) -> str | None:
    """
    renderedFields.description содержит HTML (если expand=renderedFields).
    """
    rendered = issue.get("renderedFields", {})
    return rendered.get("description")  # HTML-строка или None


# ── Пример использования ─────────────────────────────────────────────
if __name__ == "__main__":
    from confluence_parser import find_by_keyword, print_results  # наш прошлый скрипт

    # Найти задачи в проекте DATA, созданные в этом году
    issues = search_issues(
        jql='project = DATA AND created >= "2025-01-01" ORDER BY updated DESC',
        fields=["summary", "description", "status"],
        max_results=100,
    )

    keyword = "batch_size"

    for issue in issues:
        html = get_issue_html(issue)
        if not html:
            continue

        results = find_by_keyword(html, keyword)
        if results:
            key     = issue["key"]
            summary = issue["fields"]["summary"]
            print(f"\n{'='*60}")
            print(f"[{key}] {summary}")
            print_results(results, keyword)









Jira v2 api

import requests
from requests.auth import HTTPBasicAuth
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup

JIRA_URL = "https://your-domain.atlassian.net"
EMAIL    = "you@example.com"
TOKEN    = "your_api_token"

auth    = HTTPBasicAuth(EMAIL, TOKEN)
headers = {"Accept": "application/json", "Content-Type": "application/json"}


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 1. ПОИСК ЗАДАЧ (summary + description)
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def search_issues(keyword: str, max_results: int = 50) -> list[dict]:
    """
    text~ ищет по summary, description, comments, environment
    и всем текстовым custom fields одновременно.
    """
    url = f"{JIRA_URL}/rest/api/2/search"
    issues = []
    start_at = 0

    while True:
        payload = {
            "jql": f'text ~ "{keyword}" ORDER BY updated DESC',
            "startAt": start_at,
            "maxResults": max_results,
            "fields": [
                "summary", "description", "status", "assignee",
                "reporter", "project", "issuetype", "comment",
                "attachment", "labels", "environment",
            ],
            "expand": ["renderedFields"],
        }

        resp = requests.post(url, headers=headers, auth=auth, json=payload)
        resp.raise_for_status()
        data = resp.json()
        batch = data.get("issues", [])
        issues.extend(batch)

        # Пагинация
        start_at += len(batch)
        if start_at >= data.get("total", 0) or not batch:
            break

    return issues


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 2. ПОИСК В ВОРКLOGАХ
#    (text~ не покрывает worklogs — нужен отдельный запрос)
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def search_in_worklogs(issue_key: str, keyword: str) -> list[dict]:
    url = f"{JIRA_URL}/rest/api/2/issue/{issue_key}/worklog"
    resp = requests.get(url, headers=headers, auth=auth)
    resp.raise_for_status()

    matches = []
    for wl in resp.json().get("worklogs", []):
        comment = wl.get("comment", "")
        if keyword.lower() in comment.lower():
            matches.append({
                "author":     wl.get("author", {}).get("displayName"),
                "started":    wl.get("started", "")[:10],
                "time_spent": wl.get("timeSpent"),
                "comment":    comment,
            })
    return matches


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 3. ПОИСК В ATTACHMENTS (по имени файла)
#    Содержимое файлов API не индексирует — только имена
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def search_in_attachments(issue: dict, keyword: str) -> list[dict]:
    attachments = issue.get("fields", {}).get("attachment", [])
    return [
        {
            "filename": a["filename"],
            "mime_type": a["mimeType"],
            "url": a["content"],
            "author": a.get("author", {}).get("displayName"),
        }
        for a in attachments
        if keyword.lower() in a["filename"].lower()
    ]


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 4. ИЗВЛЕЧЕНИЕ ВХОЖДЕНИЙ ИЗ HTML-ПОЛЕЙ
#    (description, environment, comment — приходят как HTML)
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def extract_context_from_html(html: str, keyword: str) -> list[str]:
    """Возвращает список абзацев/ячеек содержащих keyword."""
    if not html:
        return []
    soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "html.parser")
    matches = []

    for el in soup.find_all(["p", "li", "td", "th", "h1", "h2", "h3", "pre"]):
        text = el.get_text(separator=" ", strip=True)
        if keyword.lower() in text.lower():
            highlighted = text.replace(keyword, f"[{keyword.upper()}]")
            tag = el.name.upper()
            matches.append(f"[{tag}] {highlighted}")

    return matches


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 5. СБОРКА ПОЛНОГО РЕЗУЛЬТАТА ПО ЗАДАЧЕ
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def analyze_issue(issue: dict, keyword: str, include_worklogs: bool = True) -> dict | None:
    key     = issue["key"]
    fields  = issue["fields"]
    rendered = issue.get("renderedFields", {})

    result = {
        "key":       key,
        "summary":   fields.get("summary", ""),
        "project":   fields.get("project", {}).get("name"),
        "status":    fields.get("status", {}).get("name"),
        "assignee":  (fields.get("assignee") or {}).get("displayName", "—"),
        "reporter":  (fields.get("reporter") or {}).get("displayName", "—"),
        "areas":     {},
    }

    # ── Summary ──────────────────────────────────────────
    if keyword.lower() in result["summary"].lower():
        result["areas"]["summary"] = result["summary"]

    # ── Description ──────────────────────────────────────
    desc_html = rendered.get("description", "")
    desc_hits = extract_context_from_html(desc_html, keyword)
    if desc_hits:
        result["areas"]["description"] = desc_hits

    # ── Environment ──────────────────────────────────────
    env_html = rendered.get("environment", "")
    env_hits = extract_context_from_html(env_html, keyword)
    if env_hits:
        result["areas"]["environment"] = env_hits

    # ── Labels ───────────────────────────────────────────
    label_hits = [l for l in fields.get("labels", []) if keyword.lower() in l.lower()]
    if label_hits:
        result["areas"]["labels"] = label_hits

    # ── Comments ─────────────────────────────────────────
    comment_hits = []
    comments = rendered.get("comment", {}).get("comments", [])
    for c in comments:
        hits = extract_context_from_html(c.get("renderedBody", ""), keyword)
        if hits:
            comment_hits.append({
                "author":  c.get("author", {}).get("displayName"),
                "created": c.get("created", "")[:10],
                "hits":    hits,
            })
    if comment_hits:
        result["areas"]["comments"] = comment_hits

    # ── Attachments ──────────────────────────────────────
    att_hits = search_in_attachments(issue, keyword)
    if att_hits:
        result["areas"]["attachments"] = att_hits

    # ── Worklogs ─────────────────────────────────────────
    if include_worklogs:
        wl_hits = search_in_worklogs(key, keyword)
        if wl_hits:
            result["areas"]["worklogs"] = wl_hits

    # Если нет ни одного совпадения — пропускаем
    if not result["areas"]:
        return None

    return result


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 6. ВЫВОД РЕЗУЛЬТАТОВ
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

AREA_LABELS = {
    "summary":     "Summary",
    "description": "Description",
    "environment": "Environment",
    "labels":      "Labels",
    "comments":    "Comments",
    "attachments": "Attachments",
    "worklogs":    "Worklogs",
}

def print_results(results: list[dict], keyword: str):
    if not results:
        print(f'Ничего не найдено по запросу "{keyword}"')
        return

    print(f'\nРезультаты поиска "{keyword}": {len(results)} задач(и)\n')

    for r in results:
        print(f"{'='*65}")
        print(f"  {r['key']}  |  {r['summary']}")
        print(f"  Проект: {r['project']}  |  Статус: {r['status']}")
        print(f"  Исполнитель: {r['assignee']}  |  Репортер: {r['reporter']}")
        print(f"  Области: {', '.join(AREA_LABELS[a] for a in r['areas'])}")
        print()

        for area, data in r["areas"].items():
            label = AREA_LABELS[area]
            print(f"  ▌ {label}")

            if area == "summary":
                print(f"    {data}")

            elif area in ("description", "environment"):
                for hit in data:
                    print(f"    {hit}")

            elif area == "labels":
                print(f"    {', '.join(data)}")

            elif area == "comments":
                for c in data:
                    print(f"    [{c['created']}] {c['author']}:")
                    for hit in c["hits"]:
                        print(f"      {hit}")

            elif area == "attachments":
                for a in data:
                    print(f"    {a['filename']} ({a['mime_type']}) — {a['author']}")

            elif area == "worklogs":
                for w in data:
                    print(f"    [{w['started']}] {w['author']} ({w['time_spent']}): {w['comment'][:120]}")

            print()

        print()


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# ТОЧКА ВХОДА
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

if __name__ == "__main__":
    keyword = "batch_size"

    print(f'Поиск "{keyword}" во всех областях Jira...')
    issues  = search_issues(keyword)
    results = [r for i in issues if (r := analyze_issue(i, keyword)) is not None]
    print_results(results, keyword)








# ── Comments ─────────────────────────────────────────────
comment_hits = []
comments = rendered.get("comment", {}).get("comments", [])
for c in comments:
    body_html = c.get("renderedBody", "")
    hits = extract_context_from_html(body_html, keyword)
    if hits:
        # Извлекаем весь текст комментария целиком
        full_text = BeautifulSoup(body_html, "html.parser").get_text(
            separator="\n", strip=True
        )
        comment_hits.append({
            "author":    c.get("author", {}).get("displayName"),
            "created":   c.get("created", "")[:10],
            "hits":      hits,
            "full_text": full_text,   # ← добавили
        })
if comment_hits:
    result["areas"]["comments"] = comment_hits




elif area == "comments":
    for c in data:
        print(f"    [{c['created']}] {c['author']}:")
        # Подсвечиваем keyword в полном тексте
        full = c["full_text"].replace(keyword, f"[{keyword.upper()}]")
        # Отступ для каждой строки
        for line in full.splitlines():
            if line.strip():
                print(f"      {line}")
        print()









import re

def is_exact_match(text: str, keyword: str) -> bool:
    """
    Проверяет что keyword встречается как отдельное слово/идентификатор,
    а не как часть другого слова.
    Граница: пробел, пунктуация, начало/конец строки.
    """
    # \b не работает для snake_case идентификаторов с _ на границе,
    # поэтому используем явные разделители
    pattern = r'(?<![a-zA-Z0-9_])' + re.escape(keyword) + r'(?![a-zA-Z0-9_])'
    return bool(re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE))

def extract_context_from_html(html: str, keyword: str) -> list[str]:
    if not html:
        return []
    soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "html.parser")
    matches = []

    for el in soup.find_all(["p", "li", "td", "th", "h1", "h2", "h3", "pre"]):
        text = el.get_text(separator=" ", strip=True)
        if is_exact_match(text, keyword):       # ← было: keyword.lower() in text.lower()
            highlighted = re.sub(
                r'(?<![a-zA-Z0-9_])' + re.escape(keyword) + r'(?![a-zA-Z0-9_])',
                f"[{keyword.upper()}]",
                text,
                flags=re.IGNORECASE,
            )
            tag = el.name.upper()
            matches.append(f"[{tag}] {highlighted}")

    return matches


# В analyze_issue:

# Summary
if is_exact_match(result["summary"], keyword):      # ← было: keyword.lower() in ...
    result["areas"]["summary"] = result["summary"]

# Labels
label_hits = [l for l in fields.get("labels", []) if is_exact_match(l, keyword)]

# В search_in_worklogs:
if is_exact_match(comment, keyword):                # ← было: keyword.lower() in ...






# ── Comments ─────────────────────────────────────────────
comment_hits = []
comments = rendered.get("comment", {}).get("comments", [])
for c in comments:
    body_html = c.get("renderedBody", "")
    
    # Сначала проверяем точное совпадение во всём тексте комментария
    full_text = BeautifulSoup(body_html, "html.parser").get_text(
        separator="\n", strip=True
    )
    
    if not is_exact_match(full_text, keyword):   # ← отсекаем like-совпадения
        continue
    
    # Если прошли фильтр — собираем подсвеченные фрагменты
    hits = extract_context_from_html(body_html, keyword)
    if hits:
        comment_hits.append({
            "author":    c.get("author", {}).get("displayName"),
            "created":   c.get("created", "")[:10],
            "hits":      hits,
            "full_text": full_text,
        })

if comment_hits:
    result["areas"]["comments"] = comment_hits


# ── Description ──────────────────────────────────────────
desc_html = rendered.get("description", "")
desc_full = BeautifulSoup(desc_html, "html.parser").get_text(separator=" ", strip=True)

if is_exact_match(desc_full, keyword):           # ← pre-check на весь текст
    desc_hits = extract_context_from_html(desc_html, keyword)
    if desc_hits:
        result["areas"]["description"] = desc_hits

# ── Environment ──────────────────────────────────────────
env_html = rendered.get("environment", "")
env_full = BeautifulSoup(env_html, "html.parser").get_text(separator=" ", strip=True)

if is_exact_match(env_full, keyword):            # ← pre-check на весь текст
    env_hits = extract_context_from_html(env_html, keyword)
    if env_hits:
        result["areas"]["environment"] = env_hits









Doris http upload

import json
import requests

DORIS_HOST = "http://your-doris-host:8030"
DB = "your_db"
TABLE = "your_table"
USER = "user"
PASSWORD = "password"

def stream_load(batch):
    url = f"{DORIS_HOST}/api/{DB}/{TABLE}/_stream_load"

    headers = {
        "label": f"batch_{int(__import__('time').time() * 1000)}",
        "format": "json",
        "strip_outer_array": "true"
    }

    data = json.dumps(batch)

    response = requests.put(
        url,
        headers=headers,
        data=data,
        auth=(USER, PASSWORD)
    )

    print(response.text)


from kafka import KafkaConsumer

consumer = KafkaConsumer(
    'your_topic',
    bootstrap_servers=['localhost:9092'],
    value_deserializer=lambda x: json.loads(x.decode('utf-8'))
)

batch = []
BATCH_SIZE = 10000

for msg in consumer:
    batch.append(msg.value)

    if len(batch) >= BATCH_SIZE:
        stream_load(batch)
        batch.clear()







import sqlglot
from sqlglot import exp
from collections import Counter
from typing import List

def extract_tables(sql: str, dialect: str = "starrocks") -> List[str]:
    """Извлекает все таблицы из SQL запроса в формате db.table"""
    try:
        statements = sqlglot.parse(sql, dialect=dialect)
    except Exception:
        return []

    tables = []
    for statement in statements:
        if statement is None:
            continue
        for table in statement.find_all(exp.Table):
            # Пропускаем CTE-алиасы (сами определения WITH x AS (...))
            if isinstance(table.parent, exp.CTE):
                continue

            db = table.args.get("db")
            name = table.name

            if not name:
                continue

            if db:
                tables.append(f"{db.name}.{name}")
            else:
                tables.append(name)

    return tables


def count_tables(queries: List[str], dialect: str = "starrocks") -> Counter:
    """Считает сколько раз каждая таблица встречается во всех запросах"""
    counter = Counter()
    for sql in queries:
        tables = extract_tables(sql, dialect=dialect)
        counter.update(tables)
    return counter


# ─── Demo ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
if __name__ == "__main__":
    queries = [
        # JOIN + subquery
        """
        SELECT a.id, b.name
        FROM db1.orders a
        JOIN db1.customers b ON a.customer_id = b.id
        WHERE a.id IN (SELECT id FROM db2.blacklist)
        """,
        # CTE + nested CTE
        """
        WITH
          recent AS (
            SELECT * FROM db1.orders WHERE dt >= '2024-01-01'
          ),
          enriched AS (
            SELECT r.*, p.title
            FROM recent r
            JOIN db2.products p ON r.product_id = p.id
          )
        SELECT * FROM enriched
        """,
        # Без схемы
        """
        SELECT * FROM orders o
        JOIN customers c ON o.customer_id = c.id
        """,
        # Повтор таблицы из первого запроса
        """
        SELECT * FROM db1.orders WHERE status = 'new'
        """,
    ]

    print("=" * 55)
    print(f"{'Таблица':<35} {'Кол-во':>8}")
    print("=" * 55)

    counts = count_tables(queries)
    for table, cnt in counts.most_common():
        print(f"{table:<35} {cnt:>8}")

    print("=" * 55)
    print(f"Уникальных таблиц: {len(counts)}")






import pymysql
import sqlglot
from sqlglot import exp
from collections import Counter
from typing import Iterator
import logging

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s")
log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# ─── Config ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

DORIS_CONFIG = {
    "host": "your_doris_host",
    "port": 9030,
    "user": "your_user",
    "password": "your_password",
    "database": "your_database",
    "charset": "utf8mb4",
    "connect_timeout": 30,
}

FETCH_SQL = """
    SELECT query_md5, query_text
    FROM your_table
    WHERE query_text IS NOT NULL
      AND LENGTH(query_text) > 10
    ORDER BY query_md5
"""

BATCH_SIZE = 2_000       # строк за один fetchmany
LOG_EVERY  = 50_000      # логировать прогресс каждые N строк

# ─── Streaming fetch ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def iter_queries(config: dict, sql: str, batch_size: int) -> Iterator[tuple[str, str]]:
    """Стримит строки (query_md5, query_text) батчами, не грузя всё в память."""
    conn = pymysql.connect(**config)
    try:
        # SSCursor — серверный курсор, строки идут по сети порциями
        with conn.cursor(pymysql.cursors.SSCursor) as cur:
            log.info("Выполняю запрос к Doris...")
            cur.execute(sql)
            log.info("Запрос принят, начинаю стриминг")

            while True:
                rows = cur.fetchmany(batch_size)
                if not rows:
                    break
                yield from rows
    finally:
        conn.close()


# ─── Table extraction ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def extract_tables(sql: str, dialect: str = "starrocks") -> list[str]:
    try:
        statements = sqlglot.parse(sql, dialect=dialect, error_level=sqlglot.ErrorLevel.IGNORE)
    except Exception:
        return []

    tables = []
    for stmt in statements:
        if stmt is None:
            continue
        for table in stmt.find_all(exp.Table):
            if isinstance(table.parent, exp.CTE):
                continue
            db   = table.args.get("db")
            name = table.name
            if not name:
                continue
            tables.append(f"{db.name}.{name}" if db else name)
    return tables


# ─── Main ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def main():
    counter   = Counter()
    processed = 0
    errors    = 0

    for query_md5, query_text in iter_queries(DORIS_CONFIG, FETCH_SQL, BATCH_SIZE):
        processed += 1

        tables = extract_tables(query_text)
        if tables:
            counter.update(tables)
        else:
            errors += 1

        if processed % LOG_EVERY == 0:
            log.info(f"Обработано: {processed:,} | уникальных таблиц: {len(counter):,} | ошибок парсинга: {errors:,}")

    log.info(f"Итого: {processed:,} запросов | {len(counter):,} уникальных таблиц | {errors:,} ошибок")

    print("\n" + "=" * 55)
    print(f"{'Таблица':<35} {'Кол-во':>8}")
    print("=" * 55)
    for table, cnt in counter.most_common(50):
        print(f"{table:<35} {cnt:>8}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()






import pymysql
import sqlglot
from sqlglot import exp
from collections import Counter
from typing import Iterator
import logging

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s")
log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# ─── Config ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

DORIS_CONFIG = {
    "host": "your_doris_host",
    "port": 9030,
    "user": "your_user",
    "password": "your_password",
    "database": "your_database",
    "charset": "utf8mb4",
    "connect_timeout": 30,
}

FETCH_SQL = """
    SELECT query_md5, query_text
    FROM your_table
    WHERE query_text IS NOT NULL
      AND LENGTH(query_text) > 10
    ORDER BY query_md5
"""

# DDL для целевой таблицы:
# CREATE TABLE table_usage_stats (
#     db     VARCHAR(256),
#     name   VARCHAR(256),
#     cnt    BIGINT       REPLACE DEFAULT "0",
# ) ENGINE=OLAP
# AGGREGATE KEY(db, name)
# DISTRIBUTED BY HASH(name) BUCKETS 4
# PROPERTIES ("replication_num" = "1");

TARGET_TABLE = "table_usage_stats"

FETCH_BATCH  = 2_000
WRITE_BATCH  = 5_000    # строк за один INSERT
LOG_EVERY    = 50_000

# ─── Streaming fetch ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def iter_queries(config: dict, sql: str, batch_size: int) -> Iterator[tuple[str, str]]:
    conn = pymysql.connect(**config)
    try:
        with conn.cursor(pymysql.cursors.SSCursor) as cur:
            log.info("Выполняю запрос к Doris...")
            cur.execute(sql)
            log.info("Запрос принят, начинаю стриминг")
            while True:
                rows = cur.fetchmany(batch_size)
                if not rows:
                    break
                yield from rows
    finally:
        conn.close()

# ─── Table extraction ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def extract_tables(sql: str, dialect: str = "starrocks") -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    """Возвращает список (db, name). db='' если схема не указана."""
    try:
        statements = sqlglot.parse(sql, dialect=dialect, error_level=sqlglot.ErrorLevel.IGNORE)
    except Exception:
        return []

    tables = []
    for stmt in statements:
        if stmt is None:
            continue
        for table in stmt.find_all(exp.Table):
            if isinstance(table.parent, exp.CTE):
                continue
            db_node = table.args.get("db")
            name    = table.name
            if not name:
                continue
            db = db_node.name if db_node else ""
            tables.append((db, name))
    return tables

# ─── Write to Doris ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def flush_to_doris(conn: pymysql.Connection, counter: Counter, batch_size: int):
    """Сбрасывает Counter в Doris батчами через INSERT ... ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE."""
    rows    = [(db, name, cnt) for (db, name), cnt in counter.items()]
    total   = len(rows)
    written = 0

    with conn.cursor() as cur:
        for i in range(0, total, batch_size):
            batch = rows[i : i + batch_size]
            placeholders = ", ".join(["(%s, %s, %s)"] * len(batch))
            flat_values  = [v for row in batch for v in row]

            sql = f"""
                INSERT INTO {TARGET_TABLE} (db, name, cnt)
                VALUES {placeholders}
                ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE cnt = cnt + VALUES(cnt)
            """
            cur.execute(sql, flat_values)
            written += len(batch)

    conn.commit()
    log.info(f"Записано в Doris: {written:,} / {total:,} уникальных таблиц")

# ─── Main ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def main():
    counter   = Counter()
    processed = 0
    errors    = 0

    for query_md5, query_text in iter_queries(DORIS_CONFIG, FETCH_SQL, FETCH_BATCH):
        processed += 1

        tables = extract_tables(query_text)
        if tables:
            counter.update(tables)
        else:
            errors += 1

        if processed % LOG_EVERY == 0:
            log.info(f"Обработано: {processed:,} | уникальных таблиц: {len(counter):,} | ошибок парсинга: {errors:,}")

    log.info(f"Итого: {processed:,} запросов | {len(counter):,} уникальных таблиц | {errors:,} ошибок")

    # ── Запись результата ──
    write_conn = pymysql.connect(**DORIS_CONFIG)
    try:
        flush_to_doris(write_conn, counter, WRITE_BATCH)
    finally:
        write_conn.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()











import pymysql
import sqlglot
from sqlglot import exp
from collections import Counter
from typing import Iterator
import logging
import multiprocessing as mp
from functools import partial

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s")
log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

DORIS_CONFIG = {
    "host": "your_doris_host",
    "port": 9030,
    "user": "your_user",
    "password": "your_password",
    "database": "your_database",
    "charset": "utf8mb4",
    "connect_timeout": 30,
}

FETCH_SQL = """
    SELECT query_md5, query_text
    FROM your_table
    WHERE query_text IS NOT NULL
      AND LENGTH(query_text) > 10
    ORDER BY query_md5
"""

TARGET_TABLE = "table_usage_stats"

FETCH_BATCH  = 10_000   # ускорили: было 2_000
PARSE_BATCH  = 5_000    # размер чанка для одного воркера
WRITE_BATCH  = 5_000
LOG_EVERY    = 100_000
N_WORKERS    = max(1, mp.cpu_count() - 1)  # все ядра минус одно на main

# ─── Fetch ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def iter_queries(config: dict, sql: str, batch_size: int) -> Iterator[list]:
    """Отдаёт батчи строк (не по одной) — чтобы удобно скармливать в Pool."""
    conn = pymysql.connect(**config)
    try:
        with conn.cursor(pymysql.cursors.SSCursor) as cur:
            log.info("Выполняю запрос к Doris...")
            cur.execute(sql)
            log.info(f"Стриминг начат, батч={batch_size:,}, воркеров={N_WORKERS}")
            while True:
                rows = cur.fetchmany(batch_size)
                if not rows:
                    break
                yield rows          # <-- отдаём сразу весь батч
    finally:
        conn.close()

# ─── Parse (worker function — должна быть на уровне модуля для pickle) ───────

def parse_batch(rows: list) -> Counter:
    """Парсит батч строк, возвращает Counter {(db, name): cnt}."""
    counter = Counter()
    for _, query_text in rows:
        try:
            stmts = sqlglot.parse(
                query_text,
                dialect="starrocks",
                error_level=sqlglot.ErrorLevel.IGNORE
            )
        except Exception:
            continue
        for stmt in stmts:
            if stmt is None:
                continue
            for table in stmt.find_all(exp.Table):
                if isinstance(table.parent, exp.CTE):
                    continue
                db_node = table.args.get("db")
                name    = table.name
                if not name:
                    continue
                db = db_node.name if db_node else ""
                counter[(db, name)] += 1
    return counter

# ─── Write ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def flush_to_doris(conn: pymysql.Connection, counter: Counter, batch_size: int):
    rows    = [(db, name, cnt) for (db, name), cnt in counter.items()]
    total   = len(rows)
    written = 0

    with conn.cursor() as cur:
        for i in range(0, total, batch_size):
            batch        = rows[i : i + batch_size]
            placeholders = ", ".join(["(%s, %s, %s)"] * len(batch))
            flat_values  = [v for row in batch for v in row]
            cur.execute(
                f"INSERT INTO {TARGET_TABLE} (db, name, cnt) VALUES {placeholders}",
                flat_values
            )
            written += len(batch)

    conn.commit()
    log.info(f"Записано в Doris: {written:,} строк")

# ─── Main ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def main():
    global_counter = Counter()
    processed      = 0

    with mp.Pool(processes=N_WORKERS) as pool:
        for partial_counter in pool.imap_unordered(
            parse_batch,
            iter_queries(DORIS_CONFIG, FETCH_SQL, FETCH_BATCH),
            chunksize=1          # iter_queries уже батчует, chunksize=1 здесь ок
        ):
            global_counter += partial_counter
            processed      += FETCH_BATCH

            if processed % LOG_EVERY == 0:
                log.info(f"Обработано: ~{processed:,} | уникальных таблиц: {len(global_counter):,}")

    log.info(f"Парсинг завершён | уникальных таблиц: {len(global_counter):,}")

    write_conn = pymysql.connect(**DORIS_CONFIG)
    try:
        flush_to_doris(write_conn, global_counter, WRITE_BATCH)
    finally:
        write_conn.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()











import pymysql
import sqlglot
from sqlglot import exp
from collections import Counter
from typing import Iterator
import logging
import multiprocessing as mp

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s")
log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

DORIS_CONFIG = {
    "host": "your_doris_host",
    "port": 9030,
    "user": "your_user",
    "password": "your_password",
    "database": "your_database",
    "charset": "utf8mb4",
    "connect_timeout": 30,
}

SOURCE_TABLE = "your_table"
TARGET_TABLE = "table_usage_stats"

PAGE_SIZE    = 10_000   # строк за один SELECT
PARSE_BATCH  = 5_000
WRITE_BATCH  = 5_000
LOG_EVERY    = 100_000
N_WORKERS    = max(1, mp.cpu_count() - 1)

# ─── Пагинация через LIMIT/OFFSET ────────────────────────────────────────────
# Используем LIMIT + OFFSET — просто и надёжно.
# Для таблиц >10M строк лучше перейти на диапазон по ключу (см. комментарий ниже).

def get_total(config: dict) -> int:
    conn = pymysql.connect(**config)
    try:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(f"""
                SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {SOURCE_TABLE}
                WHERE query_text IS NOT NULL
                  AND LENGTH(query_text) > 10
            """)
            return cur.fetchone()[0]
    finally:
        conn.close()


def fetch_page(config: dict, offset: int, limit: int) -> list:
    """Один короткий SELECT — не держит соединение дольше нескольких секунд."""
    conn = pymysql.connect(**config)
    try:
        with conn.cursor(pymysql.cursors.Cursor) as cur:
            cur.execute(f"""
                SELECT query_md5, query_text
                FROM {SOURCE_TABLE}
                WHERE query_text IS NOT NULL
                  AND LENGTH(query_text) > 10
                ORDER BY query_md5
                LIMIT %s OFFSET %s
            """, (limit, offset))
            return cur.fetchall()
    finally:
        conn.close()


def iter_pages(config: dict, total: int, page_size: int) -> Iterator[list]:
    """Генератор страниц — каждая страница = отдельный короткий запрос."""
    for offset in range(0, total, page_size):
        rows = fetch_page(config, offset, page_size)
        if not rows:
            break
        yield rows

# ─── Parse ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def parse_batch(rows: list) -> Counter:
    counter = Counter()
    for _, query_text in rows:
        try:
            stmts = sqlglot.parse(
                query_text,
                dialect="starrocks",
                error_level=sqlglot.ErrorLevel.IGNORE
            )
        except Exception:
            continue
        for stmt in stmts:
            if stmt is None:
                continue
            for table in stmt.find_all(exp.Table):
                if isinstance(table.parent, exp.CTE):
                    continue
                db_node = table.args.get("db")
                name    = table.name
                if not name:
                    continue
                db = db_node.name if db_node else ""
                counter[(db, name)] += 1
    return counter

# ─── Write ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def flush_to_doris(conn: pymysql.Connection, counter: Counter, batch_size: int):
    rows    = [(db, name, cnt) for (db, name), cnt in counter.items()]
    total   = len(rows)
    written = 0

    with conn.cursor() as cur:
        for i in range(0, total, batch_size):
            batch        = rows[i : i + batch_size]
            placeholders = ", ".join(["(%s, %s, %s)"] * len(batch))
            flat_values  = [v for row in batch for v in row]
            cur.execute(
                f"INSERT INTO {TARGET_TABLE} (db, name, cnt) VALUES {placeholders}",
                flat_values
            )
            written += len(batch)

    conn.commit()
    log.info(f"Записано в Doris: {written:,} строк")

# ─── Main ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def main():
    log.info("Считаю общее количество строк...")
    total = get_total(DORIS_CONFIG)
    log.info(f"Всего строк: {total:,} | страниц: {total // PAGE_SIZE + 1:,} | воркеров: {N_WORKERS}")

    global_counter = Counter()
    processed      = 0

    with mp.Pool(processes=N_WORKERS) as pool:
        for partial_counter in pool.imap_unordered(
            parse_batch,
            iter_pages(DORIS_CONFIG, total, PAGE_SIZE),
            chunksize=1
        ):
            global_counter += partial_counter
            processed      += PAGE_SIZE

            if processed % LOG_EVERY == 0:
                log.info(f"Обработано: ~{processed:,}/{total:,} | уникальных таблиц: {len(global_counter):,}")

    log.info(f"Парсинг завершён | уникальных таблиц: {len(global_counter):,}")

    write_conn = pymysql.connect(**DORIS_CONFIG)
    try:
        flush_to_doris(write_conn, global_counter, WRITE_BATCH)
    finally:
        write_conn.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import pymysql
import sqlglot
from sqlglot import exp
from collections import Counter
from typing import Iterator
