# pipeline/base_stage.py
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

@dataclass
class StageResult:
    success: bool
    data: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    error: str = None
    skipped: bool = False

class BaseStage(ABC):
    
    # Переопределяется в каждом этапе
    name: str = None
    required: bool = True        # False = ошибка не ломает пайплайн
    depends_on: list[str] = []   # имена этапов, которые должны быть выполнены
    
    @abstractmethod
    def execute(self, table_id: str, context: dict) -> StageResult:
        """
        context — словарь, который накапливается по ходу пайплайна.
        Каждый этап может читать результаты предыдущих и писать свои.
        """
        ...

    def should_skip(self, table_id: str, context: dict) -> bool:
        """Переопределить если нужна логика пропуска (напр. уже обогащено)"""
        return False




# pipeline/stages/dwh_enricher.py
from pipeline.base_stage import BaseStage, StageResult

# Импортируем всю существующую логику как есть
from scripts.dwh_enrichment import DwhEnrichmentLogic  # твой скрипт

class DwhEnricher(BaseStage):
    name = "dwh"
    required = True
    depends_on = []

    def __init__(self, db_config: dict):
        self._logic = DwhEnrichmentLogic(db_config)  # инициализация твоего кода

    def should_skip(self, table_id: str, context: dict) -> bool:
        # Пропускаем если уже обогащено и нет флага force
        return context.get('registry', {}).get('enriched_dwh') \
               and not context.get('force_reenrich')

    def execute(self, table_id: str, context: dict) -> StageResult:
        try:
            # Вызываем существующую логику
            result = self._logic.run(table_id)
            
            # Кладём результат в context для следующих этапов
            return StageResult(
                success=True,
                data={"dwh_meta": result}  # колонки, типы, партиции и т.д.
            )
        except Exception as e:
            return StageResult(success=False, error=str(e))




сердце пайплайна

# pipeline/runner.py
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Type
from pipeline.base_stage import BaseStage, StageResult
from registry.manager import RegistryManager

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class PipelineRunner:

    def __init__(self, registry: RegistryManager):
        self._registry = registry
        self._stages: list[BaseStage] = []

    def register_stage(self, stage: BaseStage) -> "PipelineRunner":
        """Fluent API для регистрации этапов"""
        self._stages.append(stage)
        return self

    def run(self, table_id: str, force_reenrich: bool = False) -> bool:
        logger.info(f"[{table_id}] Pipeline started")
        self._registry.set_status(table_id, 'enriching')

        # Контекст передаётся сквозь все этапы
        context = {
            'registry': self._registry.get(table_id),
            'force_reenrich': force_reenrich,
            'stages': {},       # сюда пишут результаты этапы
            'started_at': datetime.now(),
        }

        for stage in self._stages:
            stage_name = stage.name
            logger.info(f"[{table_id}] Stage [{stage_name}] starting")

            # Проверка зависимостей
            if not self._deps_satisfied(stage, context):
                msg = f"Dependencies not met: {stage.depends_on}"
                logger.error(f"[{table_id}] Stage [{stage_name}] {msg}")
                if stage.required:
                    self._registry.set_status(table_id, 'degraded', error=msg)
                    return False
                continue

            # Пропуск
            if stage.should_skip(table_id, context):
                logger.info(f"[{table_id}] Stage [{stage_name}] skipped")
                context['stages'][stage_name] = {}
                continue

            # Выполнение
            t0 = datetime.now()
            result: StageResult = stage.execute(table_id, context)
            duration_ms = int((datetime.now() - t0).total_seconds() * 1000)

            # Логируем в БД
            self._registry.log_event(
                table_id, stage_name,
                'success' if result.success else 'failed',
                duration_ms, result.data or {'error': result.error}
            )

            if result.success:
                context['stages'][stage_name] = result.data
                self._registry.mark_stage_done(table_id, stage_name)
                logger.info(f"[{table_id}] Stage [{stage_name}] OK ({duration_ms}ms)")
            else:
                logger.error(f"[{table_id}] Stage [{stage_name}] FAILED: {result.error}")
                if stage.required:
                    self._registry.set_status(table_id, 'degraded', error=result.error)
                    return False
                # Необязательный этап — продолжаем
                context['stages'][stage_name] = {}

        self._registry.set_status(table_id, 'ready')
        logger.info(f"[{table_id}] Pipeline completed successfully")
        return True

    def _deps_satisfied(self, stage: BaseStage, context: dict) -> bool:
        return all(
            dep in context['stages']
            for dep in stage.depends_on
        )



точка входа

# main.py
import argparse
from pipeline.runner import PipelineRunner
from pipeline.stages.dwh_enricher import DwhEnricher
from pipeline.stages.confluence_enricher import ConfluenceEnricher
from pipeline.stages.jira_enricher import JiraEnricher
from pipeline.stages.llm_describer import LlmDescriber
from pipeline.stages.rag_builder import RagBuilder
from registry.manager import RegistryManager
from config import load_config

def build_pipeline(cfg: dict) -> PipelineRunner:
    registry = RegistryManager(cfg['db'])
    
    return (
        PipelineRunner(registry)
        .register_stage(DwhEnricher(cfg['dwh']))
        .register_stage(ConfluenceEnricher(cfg['confluence']))
        .register_stage(JiraEnricher(cfg['jira']))
        .register_stage(LlmDescriber(cfg['llm']))
        .register_stage(RagBuilder(cfg['doris']))
    )

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Text2SQL table pipeline')
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest='command')

    # Добавить таблицу
    add = sub.add_parser('add')
    add.add_argument('table_id', help='schema.table_name')
    add.add_argument('--force', action='store_true', help='переобогатить даже если ready')

    # Удалить таблицу
    rm = sub.add_parser('remove')
    rm.add_argument('table_id')

    # Синхронизация с DWH
    sub.add_parser('sync')

    # Ретрай деградировавших
    sub.add_parser('retry-degraded')

    args = parser.parse_args()
    cfg = load_config()
    pipeline = build_pipeline(cfg)

    if args.command == 'add':
        pipeline.registry.register(args.table_id)
        pipeline.run(args.table_id, force_reenrich=args.force)

    elif args.command == 'remove':
        pipeline.registry.remove(args.table_id)

    elif args.command == 'sync':
        pipeline.sync_with_dwh()

    elif args.command == 'retry-degraded':
        for table_id in pipeline.registry.get_by_status('degraded'):
            pipeline.run(table_id)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()










registry manager


# registry/manager.py
import pymysql
import json
import hashlib
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class RegistryManager:

    def __init__(self, db_config: dict):
        self._cfg = db_config

    def _conn(self):
        return pymysql.connect(
            **self._cfg,
            cursorclass=pymysql.cursors.DictCursor
        )

    # -------------------------
    # Жизненный цикл таблицы
    # -------------------------

    def register(self, table_id: str, priority: int = 0):
        """Добавить таблицу в реестр (idempotent)"""
        schema, table = table_id.split('.')
        with self._conn() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO text2sql_table_registry
                        (table_id, schema_name, table_name, status, priority, registered_at)
                    VALUES (%s, %s, %s, 'pending', %s, %s)
                    ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
                        status = IF(status = 'removed', 'pending', status),
                        priority = VALUES(priority)
                """, (table_id, schema, table, priority, datetime.now()))
            conn.commit()

    def remove(self, table_id: str):
        """Мягкое удаление — запись остаётся для аудита"""
        self.set_status(table_id, 'removing')

    def get(self, table_id: str) -> Optional[dict]:
        with self._conn() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    "SELECT * FROM text2sql_table_registry WHERE table_id = %s",
                    (table_id,)
                )
                return cur.fetchone()

    def get_by_status(self, status: str) -> list[str]:
        with self._conn() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    "SELECT table_id FROM text2sql_table_registry WHERE status = %s",
                    (status,)
                )
                return [r['table_id'] for r in cur.fetchall()]

    def get_active_tables(self) -> list[str]:
        """Все таблицы кроме removed"""
        with self._conn() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("""
                    SELECT table_id FROM text2sql_table_registry
                    WHERE status NOT IN ('removed', 'removing')
                """)
                return [r['table_id'] for r in cur.fetchall()]

    # -------------------------
    # Обновление состояния
    # -------------------------

    def set_status(self, table_id: str, status: str, error: str = None):
        with self._conn() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("""
                    UPDATE text2sql_table_registry
                    SET status = %s,
                        error_message = %s,
                        retry_count = IF(%s = 'enriching', retry_count + 1, retry_count)
                    WHERE table_id = %s
                """, (status, error, status, table_id))
            conn.commit()

    def mark_stage_done(self, table_id: str, stage_name: str):
        """Выставляет флаг enriched_<stage> = TRUE"""
        flag_map = {
            'dwh':        'enriched_dwh',
            'confluence': 'enriched_confluence',
            'jira':       'enriched_jira',
            'llm':        'enriched_llm',
            'rag':        'rag_chunks_built',
        }
        flag = flag_map.get(stage_name)
        if not flag:
            return
        with self._conn() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(f"""
                    UPDATE text2sql_table_registry
                    SET {flag} = TRUE,
                        last_enriched_at = %s
                    WHERE table_id = %s
                """, (datetime.now(), table_id))
            conn.commit()

    def update_ddl_hash(self, table_id: str, ddl: str):
        """Для отслеживания изменений структуры таблицы"""
        ddl_hash = hashlib.md5(ddl.encode()).hexdigest()
        with self._conn() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("""
                    UPDATE text2sql_table_registry
                    SET ddl_hash = %s WHERE table_id = %s
                """, (ddl_hash, table_id))
            conn.commit()
        return ddl_hash

    def ddl_hash_changed(self, table_id: str, current_ddl: str) -> bool:
        stored = (self.get(table_id) or {}).get('ddl_hash')
        current = hashlib.md5(current_ddl.encode()).hexdigest()
        return stored != current

    # -------------------------
    # Логирование событий
    # -------------------------

    def log_event(self, table_id: str, stage: str,
                  status: str, duration_ms: int, details: dict):
        with self._conn() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO text2sql_enrichment_log
                        (table_id, stage, status, duration_ms, details, created_at)
                    VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                """, (table_id, stage, status, duration_ms,
                      json.dumps(details, ensure_ascii=False), datetime.now()))
            conn.commit()

    # -------------------------
    # Синхронизация с DWH
    # -------------------------

    def sync_with_dwh(self, dwh_tables: set[str],
                      get_ddl_fn) -> dict:
        """
        dwh_tables  — актуальный список таблиц из DWH
        get_ddl_fn  — callable(table_id) -> str, возвращает DDL
        
        Возвращает словарь с тремя списками для логирования
        """
        registered = set(self.get_active_tables())

        to_add    = dwh_tables - registered
        to_remove = registered - dwh_tables
        to_check  = dwh_tables & registered  # проверить на изменения DDL

        changed = []
        for table_id in to_check:
            try:
                if self.ddl_hash_changed(table_id, get_ddl_fn(table_id)):
                    changed.append(table_id)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"DDL check failed for {table_id}: {e}")

        return {
            'to_add':    list(to_add),
            'to_remove': list(to_remove),
            'changed':   changed,
        }

как использовать
# Регистрация
registry.register('dwh.fact_sales')

# Runner обновляет статус
registry.set_status(table_id, 'enriching')

# После каждого этапа
registry.mark_stage_done(table_id, 'dwh')
registry.log_event(table_id, 'dwh', 'success', 340, {"rows": 150})

# Stage читает текущее состояние для should_skip()
state = registry.get(table_id)
# {'enriched_dwh': True, 'enriched_confluence': False, ...}






# config/settings.py
# Все креды и настройки в одном месте

from dataclasses import dataclass, field


@dataclass
class RegistryDbConfig:
    """БД где хранится реестр таблиц и логи пайплайна"""
    host: str
    port: int
    user: str
    password: str
    database: str
    charset: str = "utf8mb4"


@dataclass
class DwhDbConfig:
    """StarRocks / источник метаданных таблиц"""
    host: str
    port: int
    user: str
    password: str
    database: str
    charset: str = "utf8mb4"


@dataclass
class JiraConfig:
    base_url: str        # https://jira.company.com
    user: str            # email или логин
    token: str           # API token
    project_key: str     # PROJECT
    timeout: int = 30


@dataclass
class LlmConfig:
    base_url: str        # http://localhost:11434/v1  (ollama) или OpenAI-совместимый
    api_key: str
    model: str           # llama3, gpt-4o-mini и т.д.
    temperature: float = 0.1
    max_tokens: int = 1024
    timeout: int = 60


@dataclass
class AppConfig:
    registry_db: RegistryDbConfig
    dwh_db: DwhDbConfig
    jira: JiraConfig
    llm: LlmConfig


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Конкретные значения — меняй здесь или вынеси в .env
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------

def load_config() -> AppConfig:
    return AppConfig(
        registry_db=RegistryDbConfig(
            host="localhost",
            port=9030,
            user="registry_user",
            password="registry_pass",
            database="text2sql_meta",
        ),
        dwh_db=DwhDbConfig(
            host="starrocks-host",
            port=9030,
            user="dwh_reader",
            password="dwh_pass",
            database="dwh",
        ),
        jira=JiraConfig(
            base_url="https://jira.company.com",
            user="svc_text2sql@company.com",
            token="ATATT3xFfGF0...",
            project_key="DWH",
        ),
        llm=LlmConfig(
            base_url="http://localhost:11434/v1",
            api_key="ollama",           # для ollama любое значение
            model="llama3.1:8b",
            temperature=0.1,
            max_tokens=1024,
        ),
    )

    PipelineRunner(registry)

def cmd_add(args, registry: RegistryManager, runner: PipelineRunner):
“”“Зарегистрировать таблицу в реестре”””
registry.register(args.table_id, priority=args.priority)
print(f”✓ Registered: {args.table_id}”)

```
if args.run:
    # Сразу запустить пайплайн не через очередь, а напрямую
    logger.info(f"Starting pipeline immediately for {args.table_id}")
    runner.run_for(args.table_id)
```

def cmd_run(args, registry: RegistryManager, runner: PipelineRunner):
“”“Обработать одну PENDING таблицу”””
table_id = runner.run_next()
if table_id:
print(f”✓ Processed: {table_id}”)
else:
print(“Queue is empty — nothing to process”)

def cmd_run_all(args, registry: RegistryManager, runner: PipelineRunner):
“”“Обработать все PENDING таблицы последовательно”””
processed = 0
while True:
table_id = runner.run_next()
if table_id is None:
break
processed += 1
print(f”✓ Processed {processed} table(s)”)

def cmd_retry_degraded(args, registry: RegistryManager, runner: PipelineRunner):
“””
Повторить все DEGRADED таблицы.
resume_from_stage сохранён в реестре — пайплайн продолжится с упавшего этапа.
“””
degraded = registry.get_by_status(“degraded”)
if not degraded:
print(“No degraded tables found”)
return

```
print(f"Found {len(degraded)} degraded table(s): {degraded}")
for table_id in degraded:
    # Сбрасываем в pending — claim_pending подхватит
    registry.reset_to_pending(table_id)

# Обрабатываем все
processed = 0
while True:
    table_id = runner.run_next()
    if table_id is None:
        break
    processed += 1
print(f"✓ Retried {processed} table(s)")
```

def cmd_status(args, registry: RegistryManager, runner: PipelineRunner):
“”“Показать состояние конкретной таблицы”””
row = registry.get(args.table_id)
if not row:
print(f”Table {args.table_id} not found in registry”)
return

print(f"\n{'─'*50}")
print(f"  Table:          {row['table_id']}")

print(f"  Retry count:    {row['retry_count']}")

print(f"  Error:          {row.get('error_message') or '—'}")

```

def cmd_list_pending(args, registry: RegistryManager, runner: PipelineRunner):
“”“Список таблиц в очереди”””

def main():
parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
description=“Text2SQL table enrichment pipeline”,
formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
epilog=”””
Examples:
python main.py add dwh.fact_sales
python main.py add dwh.fact_sales –run
python main.py run
python main.py run-all
python main.py retry-degraded
python main.py status dwh.fact_sales
python main.py list-pending
“””,
)
sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=“command”, required=True)

```
# add
p_add = sub.add_parser("add", help="Register a table in the registry")
p_add.add_argument("table_id", help="schema.table_name")
p_add.add_argument("--priority", type=int, default=0,
                   help="Processing priority (higher = sooner)")
p_add.add_argument("--run", action="store_true",
                   help="Start pipeline immediately after registration")

# run
sub.add_parser("run", help="Process one PENDING table")

# run-all
sub.add_parser("run-all", help="Process all PENDING tables")

# retry-degraded
sub.add_parser("retry-degraded", help="Retry all DEGRADED tables from failed stage")

# status
p_status = sub.add_parser("status", help="Show table status")
p_status.add_argument("table_id", help="schema.table_name")

# list-pending
sub.add_parser("list-pending", help="List pending tables")

args = parser.parse_args()

cfg = load_config()
registry, runner = build_pipeline(cfg)

commands = {
    "add":             cmd_add,
    "run":             cmd_run,
    "run-all":         cmd_run_all,
    "retry-degraded":  cmd_retry_degraded,
    "status":          cmd_status,
    "list-pending":    cmd_list_pending,
}
commands[args.command](args, registry, runner)
```

if **name** == “**main**”:
main()



# pipeline/runner.py

import logging
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional

from pipeline.base_stage import BaseStage, StageResult
from registry.manager import RegistryManager

logger = logging.getLogger(**name**)

class PipelineRunner:

```
def __init__(self, registry: RegistryManager):
    self._registry = registry
    self._stages: list[BaseStage] = []

def register_stage(self, stage: BaseStage) -> "PipelineRunner":
    """Fluent API — порядок вызовов определяет порядок выполнения"""
    self._stages.append(stage)
    return self

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# Главный метод — обработать одну PENDING таблицу
# ------------------------------------------------------------------

def run_next(self) -> Optional[str]:
    """
    Атомарно захватывает одну PENDING таблицу и прогоняет по пайплайну.
    Возвращает table_id если была обработана, None если очередь пуста.
    """
    table_id = self._registry.claim_pending()
    if not table_id:
        logger.info("No pending tables in queue")
        return None

    self._run(table_id)
    return table_id

def run_for(self, table_id: str):
    """Запустить пайплайн для конкретной таблицы (уже должна быть в реестре)"""
    self._run(table_id)

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# Внутренняя логика
# ------------------------------------------------------------------

def _run(self, table_id: str):
    logger.info(f"[{table_id}] ===== Pipeline started =====")
    started_at = datetime.now()

    # Читаем текущее состояние из реестра
    # registry_row содержит флаги stage_<name>_done и resume_from_stage
    registry_row = self._registry.get(table_id)
    if not registry_row:
        logger.error(f"[{table_id}] Not found in registry, aborting")
        return

    resume_from = registry_row.get("resume_from_stage")
    if resume_from:
        logger.info(f"[{table_id}] Resuming from stage [{resume_from}]")

    skipping = bool(resume_from)  # пропускаем этапы до resume_from

    for stage in self._stages:
        stage_name = stage.name

        # Режим возобновления: пропускаем всё до нужного этапа
        if skipping:
            if stage_name == resume_from:
                skipping = False  # дошли до аварийного этапа — начинаем
            else:
                # Но дополнительно проверяем флаг — вдруг этап уже выполнен
                if stage.should_skip(registry_row):
                    logger.info(f"[{table_id}] Stage [{stage_name}] already done, skipping")
                    continue
                # Этап до resume_from, но не выполнен — нештатная ситуация,
                # запускаем его
                logger.warning(
                    f"[{table_id}] Stage [{stage_name}] is before resume point "
                    f"but not done — executing anyway"
                )

        # Обычный пропуск уже выполненных этапов (при повторном запуске)
        if not skipping and stage.should_skip(registry_row):
            logger.info(f"[{table_id}] Stage [{stage_name}] already done, skipping")
            self._registry.log_event(table_id, stage_name, "skipped")
            continue

        # --- Выполнение этапа ---
        logger.info(f"[{table_id}] Stage [{stage_name}] starting")
        self._registry.log_event(table_id, stage_name, "started")
        t0 = datetime.now()

        try:
            result: StageResult = stage.execute(table_id)
        except Exception as e:
            # Неожиданное исключение вне StageResult
            result = StageResult(success=False, error=f"Unhandled exception: {e}")
            logger.exception(f"[{table_id}] Stage [{stage_name}] raised exception")

        duration_ms = int((datetime.now() - t0).total_seconds() * 1000)

        if result.success:
            self._registry.mark_stage_done(table_id, stage_name)
            self._registry.log_event(
                table_id, stage_name, "success",
                duration_ms, result.details
            )
            logger.info(
                f"[{table_id}] Stage [{stage_name}] OK "
                f"({duration_ms}ms) {result.details}"
            )
        else:
            self._registry.log_event(
                table_id, stage_name, "failed",
                duration_ms, {"error": result.error}
            )
            logger.error(f"[{table_id}] Stage [{stage_name}] FAILED: {result.error}")

            if stage.required:
                # Запоминаем с какого этапа возобновить при следующем запуске
                self._registry.mark_failed(table_id, stage_name, result.error)
                total_sec = (datetime.now() - started_at).total_seconds()
                logger.info(
                    f"[{table_id}] ===== Pipeline FAILED "
                    f"at [{stage_name}] after {total_sec:.1f}s ====="
                )
                return
            else:
                # Необязательный этап — логируем и продолжаем
                logger.warning(
                    f"[{table_id}] Stage [{stage_name}] is not required, continuing"
                )
                # Помечаем как done чтобы не повторять при resume
                self._registry.mark_stage_done(table_id, stage_name)

    # Все этапы пройдены
    self._registry.mark_ready(table_id)
    total_sec = (datetime.now() - started_at).total_seconds()
    logger.info(
        f"[{table_id}] ===== Pipeline COMPLETED in {total_sec:.1f}s ====="
    )
```



# registry/manager.py

import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional

import pymysql
import pymysql.cursors

logger = logging.getLogger(**name**)

class RegistryManager:

```
def __init__(self, db_config):
    self._cfg = db_config

def _conn(self):
    return pymysql.connect(
        host=self._cfg.host,
        port=self._cfg.port,
        user=self._cfg.user,
        password=self._cfg.password,
        database=self._cfg.database,
        charset=self._cfg.charset,
        cursorclass=pymysql.cursors.DictCursor,
        autocommit=False,
    )

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# DDL — создание таблиц если не существуют
# ------------------------------------------------------------------

def ensure_tables(self):
    """Создать служебные таблицы при первом запуске"""
    ddl_registry = """
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS text2sql_table_registry (
        table_id            VARCHAR(255)    NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
        schema_name         VARCHAR(128)    NOT NULL,
        table_name          VARCHAR(128)    NOT NULL,
        status              VARCHAR(32)     NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
        -- статусы: pending | enriching | ready | degraded | removing | removed

        -- флаги выполненных этапов
        stage_dwh_done      BOOLEAN         NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
        stage_jira_done     BOOLEAN         NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
        stage_llm_done      BOOLEAN         NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,

        -- с какого этапа возобновить при ошибке
        resume_from_stage   VARCHAR(64)     NULL,

        priority            INT             NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        retry_count         INT             NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        error_message       TEXT            NULL,

        registered_at       DATETIME        NOT NULL,
        last_updated_at     DATETIME        NULL,
        completed_at        DATETIME        NULL
    ) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4;
    """

    ddl_log = """
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS text2sql_enrichment_log (
        id              BIGINT          NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
        table_id        VARCHAR(255)    NOT NULL,
        stage           VARCHAR(64)     NOT NULL,
        status          VARCHAR(32)     NOT NULL,   -- started | success | failed | skipped
        duration_ms     INT             NULL,
        details         JSON            NULL,
        created_at      DATETIME        NOT NULL
    ) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4;
    """

    with self._conn() as conn:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(ddl_registry)
            cur.execute(ddl_log)
        conn.commit()
    logger.info("Registry tables ensured")

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# Жизненный цикл
# ------------------------------------------------------------------

def register(self, table_id: str, priority: int = 0):
    """Idempotent — повторная регистрация не ломает состояние"""
    schema, table = table_id.split(".", 1)
    with self._conn() as conn:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("""
                INSERT INTO text2sql_table_registry
                    (table_id, schema_name, table_name, status, priority, registered_at)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, 'pending', %s, %s)
                ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
                    status          = IF(status IN ('removed','degraded'), 'pending', status),
                    priority        = VALUES(priority),
                    resume_from_stage = NULL,
                    error_message   = NULL
            """, (table_id, schema, table, priority, datetime.now()))
        conn.commit()
    logger.info(f"Registered: {table_id}")

def claim_pending(self) -> Optional[str]:
    """
    Атомарно захватывает одну PENDING-таблицу и переводит в ENRICHING.
    Возвращает table_id или None если очередь пуста.
    """
    with self._conn() as conn:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            # Сначала ищем — с учётом приоритета и retry
            cur.execute("""
                SELECT table_id FROM text2sql_table_registry
                WHERE status = 'pending'
                ORDER BY priority DESC, registered_at ASC
                LIMIT 1
                FOR UPDATE
            """)
            row = cur.fetchone()
            if not row:
                return None

            table_id = row["table_id"]
            cur.execute("""
                UPDATE text2sql_table_registry
                SET status        = 'enriching',
                    retry_count   = retry_count + 1,
                    last_updated_at = %s
                WHERE table_id = %s
            """, (datetime.now(), table_id))
        conn.commit()
    logger.info(f"Claimed: {table_id}")
    return table_id

def get(self, table_id: str) -> Optional[dict]:
    with self._conn() as conn:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM text2sql_table_registry WHERE table_id = %s",
                (table_id,)
            )
            return cur.fetchone()

def get_by_status(self, status: str) -> list:
    with self._conn() as conn:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                "SELECT table_id FROM text2sql_table_registry WHERE status = %s",
                (status,)
            )
            return [r["table_id"] for r in cur.fetchall()]

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# Обновление состояния
# ------------------------------------------------------------------

def mark_stage_done(self, table_id: str, stage_name: str):
    """
    Отмечает этап как выполненный.
    resume_from_stage сбрасывается — при следующей ошибке
    он будет выставлен заново на упавшем этапе.
    """
    flag_col = f"stage_{stage_name}_done"
    with self._conn() as conn:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(f"""
                UPDATE text2sql_table_registry
                SET {flag_col}        = TRUE,
                    resume_from_stage = NULL,
                    last_updated_at   = %s
                WHERE table_id = %s
            """, (datetime.now(), table_id))
        conn.commit()

def mark_failed(self, table_id: str, failed_stage: str, error: str):
    """
    Переводит в DEGRADED и запоминает с какого этапа возобновить.
    """
    with self._conn() as conn:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("""
                UPDATE text2sql_table_registry
                SET status            = 'degraded',
                    resume_from_stage = %s,
                    error_message     = %s,
                    last_updated_at   = %s
                WHERE table_id = %s
            """, (failed_stage, error[:2000], datetime.now(), table_id))
        conn.commit()
    logger.error(f"[{table_id}] Marked degraded at stage [{failed_stage}]: {error}")

def mark_ready(self, table_id: str):
    with self._conn() as conn:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("""
                UPDATE text2sql_table_registry
                SET status            = 'ready',
                    resume_from_stage = NULL,
                    error_message     = NULL,
                    completed_at      = %s,
                    last_updated_at   = %s
                WHERE table_id = %s
            """, (datetime.now(), datetime.now(), table_id))
        conn.commit()

def reset_to_pending(self, table_id: str):
    """Для retry деградировавших — статус pending, resume_from_stage сохраняется"""
    with self._conn() as conn:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("""
                UPDATE text2sql_table_registry
                SET status = 'pending', last_updated_at = %s
                WHERE table_id = %s
            """, (datetime.now(), table_id))
        conn.commit()

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# Логирование
# ------------------------------------------------------------------

def log_event(self, table_id: str, stage: str,
              status: str, duration_ms: int = None, details: dict = None):
    with self._conn() as conn:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("""
                INSERT INTO text2sql_enrichment_log
                    (table_id, stage, status, duration_ms, details, created_at)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
            """, (
                table_id, stage, status, duration_ms,
                json.dumps(details or {}, ensure_ascii=False),
                datetime.now(),
            ))
        conn.commit()
```



# config/settings.py

# Все креды и настройки в одном месте

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

@dataclass
class RegistryDbConfig:
“”“БД где хранится реестр таблиц и логи пайплайна”””

“”“StarRocks / источник метаданных таблиц”””

user: str

# pipeline/base_stage.py

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

@dataclass
class StageResult:
success: bool
error: str = None
details: dict = field(default_factory=dict)   # пишется в enrichment_log
skipped: bool = False

class BaseStage(ABC):
“””
Каждый этап:
- имеет уникальное имя (совпадает с колонкой stage_<name>_done в реестре)
- сохраняет результаты в свою таблицу самостоятельно
- не передаёт данные следующим этапам через context
“””
name: str = None        # обязательно переопределить
required: bool = True   # если False — ошибка этапа не останавливает пайплайн

```
@abstractmethod
def execute(self, table_id: str) -> StageResult:
    ...

def should_skip(self, registry_row: dict) -> bool:
    """
    Пропустить если уже выполнено (для возобновления с аварийного места).
    Читает флаг stage_<name>_done из реестра.
    """
    flag = f"stage_{self.name}_done"
    return bool(registry_row.get(flag, False))
```



# pipeline/stages/dwh_enricher.py

# 

# Этап 1: обогащение метаданными из DWH (StarRocks/MySQL-compatible)

# Сохраняет результат в таблицу text2sql_dwh_meta

import logging
import pymysql
import pymysql.cursors
from datetime import datetime

from pipeline.base_stage import BaseStage, StageResult

logger = logging.getLogger(**name**)

class DwhEnricher(BaseStage):
name = “dwh”
required = True

```
def __init__(self, dwh_config, registry_config):
    self._dwh_cfg = dwh_config
    self._reg_cfg = registry_config

def _dwh_conn(self):
    return pymysql.connect(
        host=self._dwh_cfg.host,
        port=self._dwh_cfg.port,
        user=self._dwh_cfg.user,
        password=self._dwh_cfg.password,
        database=self._dwh_cfg.database,
        charset=self._dwh_cfg.charset,
        cursorclass=pymysql.cursors.DictCursor,
    )

def _reg_conn(self):
    return pymysql.connect(
        host=self._reg_cfg.host,
        port=self._reg_cfg.port,
        user=self._reg_cfg.user,
        password=self._reg_cfg.password,
        database=self._reg_cfg.database,
        charset=self._reg_cfg.charset,
        cursorclass=pymysql.cursors.DictCursor,
        autocommit=False,
    )

def _ensure_output_table(self, conn):
    with conn.cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS text2sql_dwh_meta (
                table_id        VARCHAR(255)    NOT NULL,
                column_name     VARCHAR(128)    NOT NULL,
                data_type       VARCHAR(128)    NOT NULL,
                is_nullable     BOOLEAN,
                column_comment  TEXT,
                ordinal_position INT,
                enriched_at     DATETIME        NOT NULL,
                PRIMARY KEY (table_id, column_name)
            ) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4
        """)
    conn.commit()

def execute(self, table_id: str) -> StageResult:
    schema_name, table_name = table_id.split(".", 1)

    # 1. Читаем метаданные колонок из DWH
    try:
        with self._dwh_conn() as dwh:
            with dwh.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("""
                    SELECT
                        COLUMN_NAME       AS column_name,
                        DATA_TYPE         AS data_type,
                        IS_NULLABLE       AS is_nullable,
                        COLUMN_COMMENT    AS column_comment,
                        ORDINAL_POSITION  AS ordinal_position
                    FROM information_schema.COLUMNS
                    WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = %s
                      AND TABLE_NAME   = %s
                    ORDER BY ORDINAL_POSITION
                """, (schema_name, table_name))
                columns = dwh.fetchall()
    except Exception as e:
        return StageResult(success=False, error=f"DWH read error: {e}")

    if not columns:
        return StageResult(
            success=False,
            error=f"Table {table_id} not found in DWH information_schema"
        )

    # 2. Сохраняем в свою таблицу в registry БД
    try:
        with self._reg_conn() as reg:
            self._ensure_output_table(reg)
            with reg.cursor() as cur:
                # Удаляем устаревшие данные перед вставкой
                cur.execute(
                    "DELETE FROM text2sql_dwh_meta WHERE table_id = %s",
                    (table_id,)
                )
                cur.executemany("""
                    INSERT INTO text2sql_dwh_meta
                        (table_id, column_name, data_type,
                         is_nullable, column_comment, ordinal_position, enriched_at)
                    VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                """, [
                    (
                        table_id,
                        col["column_name"],
                        col["data_type"],
                        col["is_nullable"] == "YES",
                        col.get("column_comment", ""),
                        col["ordinal_position"],
                        datetime.now(),
                    )
                    for col in columns
                ])
            reg.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        return StageResult(success=False, error=f"Registry write error: {e}")

    logger.info(f"[{table_id}] DWH: saved {len(columns)} columns")
    return StageResult(
        success=True,
        details={"columns_count": len(columns)}
    )
```



# pipeline/stages/jira_enricher.py

# 

# Этап 2: поиск связанных задач Jira по имени таблицы

# Сохраняет результат в таблицу text2sql_jira_issues

import logging
import requests
from datetime import datetime

import pymysql
import pymysql.cursors

from pipeline.base_stage import BaseStage, StageResult

logger = logging.getLogger(**name**)

class JiraEnricher(BaseStage):
name = “jira”
required = False        # Jira может быть недоступна — не блокируем пайплайн

```
def __init__(self, jira_config, registry_config):
    self._jira_cfg = jira_config
    self._reg_cfg = registry_config

def _reg_conn(self):
    return pymysql.connect(
        host=self._reg_cfg.host,
        port=self._reg_cfg.port,
        user=self._reg_cfg.user,
        password=self._reg_cfg.password,
        database=self._reg_cfg.database,
        charset=self._reg_cfg.charset,
        cursorclass=pymysql.cursors.DictCursor,
        autocommit=False,
    )

def _ensure_output_table(self, conn):
    with conn.cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS text2sql_jira_issues (
                id              BIGINT          NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
                table_id        VARCHAR(255)    NOT NULL,
                issue_key       VARCHAR(64)     NOT NULL,
                summary         TEXT,
                status          VARCHAR(64),
                issue_type      VARCHAR(64),
                description     TEXT,
                enriched_at     DATETIME        NOT NULL,
                UNIQUE KEY uq_table_issue (table_id, issue_key)
            ) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4
        """)
    conn.commit()

def _search_jira(self, table_name: str) -> list[dict]:
    """JQL-поиск задач упоминающих имя таблицы"""
    jql = (
        f'project = "{self._jira_cfg.project_key}" '
        f'AND text ~ "{table_name}" '
        f'ORDER BY updated DESC'
    )
    url = f"{self._jira_cfg.base_url}/rest/api/2/search"
    resp = requests.get(
        url,
        params={"jql": jql, "maxResults": 20,
                "fields": "summary,status,issuetype,description"},
        auth=(self._jira_cfg.user, self._jira_cfg.token),
        timeout=self._jira_cfg.timeout,
    )
    resp.raise_for_status()
    return resp.json().get("issues", [])

def execute(self, table_id: str) -> StageResult:
    _, table_name = table_id.split(".", 1)

    # 1. Запрос в Jira
    try:
        issues = self._search_jira(table_name)
    except requests.RequestException as e:
        return StageResult(success=False, error=f"Jira API error: {e}")

    # 2. Сохраняем в registry БД
    try:
        with self._reg_conn() as reg:
            self._ensure_output_table(reg)
            with reg.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    "DELETE FROM text2sql_jira_issues WHERE table_id = %s",
                    (table_id,)
                )
                if issues:
                    cur.executemany("""
                        INSERT INTO text2sql_jira_issues
                            (table_id, issue_key, summary, status, issue_type, description, enriched_at)
                        VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                    """, [
                        (
                            table_id,
                            issue["key"],
                            issue["fields"].get("summary", ""),
                            issue["fields"].get("status", {}).get("name", ""),
                            issue["fields"].get("issuetype", {}).get("name", ""),
                            (issue["fields"].get("description") or "")[:4000],
                            datetime.now(),
                        )
                        for issue in issues
                    ])
            reg.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        return StageResult(success=False, error=f"Registry write error: {e}")

    logger.info(f"[{table_id}] Jira: found {len(issues)} issues")
    return StageResult(
        success=True,
        details={"issues_found": len(issues)}
    )
```



# pipeline/stages/llm_describer.py

# 

# Этап 3: генерация описания таблицы через LLM

# Читает из text2sql_dwh_meta и text2sql_jira_issues (уже сохранены предыдущими этапами)

# Сохраняет результат в text2sql_llm_descriptions

import json
import logging
import requests
from datetime import datetime

import pymysql
import pymysql.cursors

from pipeline.base_stage import BaseStage, StageResult

logger = logging.getLogger(**name**)

PROMPT_TEMPLATE = “”“Ты эксперт по корпоративным данным. На основе информации о таблице сформируй её описание для системы Text2SQL.

Таблица: {table_id}

Колонки:
{columns_block}

Связанные задачи Jira:
{jira_block}

Сформируй JSON строго по схеме (никакого текста вне JSON):
{{
“table_description”: “краткое описание таблицы на русском (2-3 предложения)”,
“business_purpose”: “для чего используется бизнесом”,
“column_descriptions”: {{
“column_name”: “описание колонки”
}},
“typical_queries”: [“пример запроса 1”, “пример запроса 2”]
}}”””

class LlmDescriber(BaseStage):
name = “llm”
required = True

```
def __init__(self, llm_config, registry_config):
    self._llm_cfg = llm_config
    self._reg_cfg = registry_config

def _reg_conn(self):
    return pymysql.connect(
        host=self._reg_cfg.host,
        port=self._reg_cfg.port,
        user=self._reg_cfg.user,
        password=self._reg_cfg.password,
        database=self._reg_cfg.database,
        charset=self._reg_cfg.charset,
        cursorclass=pymysql.cursors.DictCursor,
        autocommit=False,
    )

def _ensure_output_table(self, conn):
    with conn.cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS text2sql_llm_descriptions (
                table_id            VARCHAR(255)    NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
                table_description   TEXT,
                business_purpose    TEXT,
                column_descriptions JSON,
                typical_queries     JSON,
                raw_response        TEXT,
                enriched_at         DATETIME        NOT NULL
            ) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4
        """)
    conn.commit()

def _load_dwh_meta(self, conn, table_id: str) -> list[dict]:
    with conn.cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute("""
            SELECT column_name, data_type, column_comment
            FROM text2sql_dwh_meta
            WHERE table_id = %s
            ORDER BY ordinal_position
        """, (table_id,))
        return cur.fetchall()

def _load_jira_issues(self, conn, table_id: str) -> list[dict]:
    with conn.cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute("""
            SELECT issue_key, summary, status, description
            FROM text2sql_jira_issues
            WHERE table_id = %s
            LIMIT 5
        """, (table_id,))
        return cur.fetchall()

def _call_llm(self, prompt: str) -> str:
    payload = {
        "model": self._llm_cfg.model,
        "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        "temperature": self._llm_cfg.temperature,
        "max_tokens": self._llm_cfg.max_tokens,
    }
    resp = requests.post(
        f"{self._llm_cfg.base_url}/chat/completions",
        headers={
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {self._llm_cfg.api_key}",
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        json=payload,
        timeout=self._llm_cfg.timeout,
    )
    resp.raise_for_status()
    return resp.json()["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]

def _parse_llm_response(self, raw: str) -> dict:
    # Убираем возможные markdown-обёртки
    cleaned = raw.strip()
    if cleaned.startswith("```"):
        cleaned = cleaned.split("```")[1]
        if cleaned.startswith("json"):
            cleaned = cleaned[4:]
    return json.loads(cleaned.strip())

def execute(self, table_id: str) -> StageResult:
    with self._reg_conn() as reg:
        self._ensure_output_table(reg)

        # 1. Загружаем данные предыдущих этапов из БД
        columns = self._load_dwh_meta(reg, table_id)
        issues  = self._load_jira_issues(reg, table_id)

    if not columns:
        return StageResult(
            success=False,
            error=f"No DWH metadata found for {table_id}. Run dwh stage first."
        )

    # 2. Строим промпт
    columns_block = "\n".join(
        f"  - {c['column_name']} ({c['data_type']})"
        + (f": {c['column_comment']}" if c.get("column_comment") else "")
        for c in columns
    )
    jira_block = "\n".join(
        f"  [{i['issue_key']}] {i['summary']} ({i['status']})"
        for i in issues
    ) or "  Связанные задачи не найдены"

    prompt = PROMPT_TEMPLATE.format(
        table_id=table_id,
        columns_block=columns_block,
        jira_block=jira_block,
    )

    # 3. Вызов LLM
    try:
        raw_response = self._call_llm(prompt)
    except Exception as e:
        return StageResult(success=False, error=f"LLM API error: {e}")

    # 4. Парсинг ответа
    try:
        parsed = self._parse_llm_response(raw_response)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
        return StageResult(
            success=False,
            error=f"LLM returned invalid JSON: {e}",
            details={"raw_response": raw_response[:500]}
        )

    # 5. Сохраняем в БД
    try:
        with self._reg_conn() as reg:
            with reg.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO text2sql_llm_descriptions
                        (table_id, table_description, business_purpose,
                         column_descriptions, typical_queries, raw_response, enriched_at)
                    VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                    ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
                        table_description   = VALUES(table_description),
                        business_purpose    = VALUES(business_purpose),
                        column_descriptions = VALUES(column_descriptions),
                        typical_queries     = VALUES(typical_queries),
                        raw_response        = VALUES(raw_response),
                        enriched_at         = VALUES(enriched_at)
                """, (
                    table_id,
                    parsed.get("table_description", ""),
                    parsed.get("business_purpose", ""),
                    json.dumps(parsed.get("column_descriptions", {}), ensure_ascii=False),
                    json.dumps(parsed.get("typical_queries", []), ensure_ascii=False),
                    raw_response[:8000],
                    datetime.now(),
                ))
            reg.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        return StageResult(success=False, error=f"Registry write error: {e}")

    logger.info(f"[{table_id}] LLM: description generated successfully")
    return StageResult(
        success=True,
        details={"prompt_len": len(prompt), "response_len": len(raw_response)}
    )
```






import hashlib
import pymysql

def get_ddl_hash(conn, table_id: str) -> str:
    schema, table = table_id.split(".", 1)
    with conn.cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute(f"SHOW CREATE TABLE `{schema}`.`{table}`")
        row = cur.fetchone()
        # StarRocks возвращает (table_name, create_statement)
        ddl = row[1] if isinstance(row, tuple) else row["Create Table"]
    return hashlib.md5(ddl.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
